  "assumptions": [
    {
      "at": "2026-10-14T13:00:16.719234+00:00",
      "reason": "noninteractive; previously unspecified edge inputs",
      "text": "Amount grammar: optional minus, ASCII digits with correctly grouped optional thousands commas, one/two fractional digits, no whitespace or plus sign. CSV rows require four fields and valid YYYY-MM-DD, aggregate amount/category errors with physical record-start line numbers. Legacy fractional cents round HALF_UP via Decimal(str(value)). Existing full suite is baseline when new Verify module absent. These clarify malformed-input edges without changing approved spec bytes."
    }
  ],
